  SEC verified a January 2016 historical manifest with 49,745 records. ClinicalTrials.gov verified 1,793 and openFDA verified 486 in their January scopes.
  Crossref’s published provider dates show 4,491 January 2016 observations.
  Published coverage windows, provider event dates and S3 collection dates have separate labels. A query window can be empty or contain records from other dates.
  The current policy start is 2016-01-01. Differences between that target and executed scopes include publisher archive floors, late or missing publication, discovery-only routes, and genuine provider/data-access blockers.

Operational changes
  Renewed the enabled historical dispatch deadline from 2026-11-23 09:25 UTC to 2026-11-28 11:57 UTC using the existing seven-day control. Source pauses remained in force.
  EODHD market history was a genuine gap. Its daily-only controller had no enabled historical worker route. The oldest row in the pre-repair published view was dated 2021-01-04.
  Restored the EODHD historical route on C7 using the existing cross-host weighted quota and independent daily and historical checkpoints. The 2016 pilot committed 979 observations in four symbol windows, including 80 January 2016 prices verified against original provider bytes.
  The first recurring market invocation on C7 verified its checkpoint, completed 23 of 24 windows and committed 5,300 observation occurrences. The active queue spans 2016-01-01 to the 2026-09-01 daily handoff. Keep unresolved price windows open for ticker and listing checks.
  Athena independently returned 80 January 2016 price rows and 976 full-year price rows from the four pilot windows, beginning 2016-01-04. All four publication receipts were verified.
  The controller and C7 share the same EODHD quota and market operation budget. Fifteen market and quota tests passed. No new credentials or provider plan were required.
  January native queue readback found 10,287 USGS earthquake records, 8,600 arXiv metadata records, and 437 NSF award records in the configured scopes. arXiv full papers and older archive vintages are separate requirements.

  Finlight pre-2021 access remains unverified. The original EDINET 2016 exclusion remains recorded. No alternate market data provider was added.

How to read the dates
  S3 date listing completed at 2026-11-21 10:57:14.772232 UTC. Boundaries describe objects present in scoped storage.
  Ingestion partitions show UTC collection days. S3 LastModified shows archive writes. Both can be later than the original provider publication date.
  Provider-original dates are the verified sample range. Discovery responses, raw envelopes and original payloads have separate labels.
  Dates exclude expired or deleted objects and raw data outside the audited prefixes. No-object entries retain their known access or implementation reason.
  Daily/historical checkpoints sampled at 2026-11-21 10:56:27.081771 UTC. Failed or blocked counts can include obsolete invocations. Read deltas and the route reasons together.
  Raw examples, Athena SQL and sample query results from 20 November are preserved in each entry. The current checks cover workers, queues and retained raw dates.
